# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

  when the extension request is valid, otherwise leaves it unchanged.
Algorithm:
  1. Receive the current date `today`.
  2. If today is on or after `borrowingDate` AND today is strictly before

  3. Otherwise (today < borrowingDate, or today >= returnDue), keep

  4. Return the (possibly updated) returnDue.

    (returnDue equals borrowingDate + 7 days at creation time).
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.

  2. Search this.members for any existing member whose
     (firstName + " " + surname) equals the candidate's full name.
  3. If validation passes and no duplicate full name exists:
       a. Create a new Member with the given firstName and surname,
          an empty borrowRecords list, and a back-reference to this
          Library.
       b. Append the new Member to this.members.
       c. Return true.
  4. Otherwise return false without modifying this.members.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::borrowBook
Summary:
  Allows this member to borrow a book identified by its title, creating
  a new BorrowRecord on success.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty, and that today
     is a valid Date.
  2. Look up the Document with the given bookTitle in
     this.library.documents. If not found, return false.
  3. If the located Document is not an instance of Book, return false.
  4. Check that no BorrowRecord currently exists in the library
     (i.e., across all Members' borrowRecords) referencing the same
     Book. If the book is already borrowed, return false.
  5. Check that this.borrowRecords.size() < 3. Otherwise return false.
  6. Create a new BorrowRecord with:
       borrowingDate = today,
       returnDue     = today + 7 days,
       book          = the located Book.
  7. Append the new BorrowRecord to this.borrowRecords.
  8. Return true.
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read and possibly appended)
  - library       : Library             (read, used to access documents
                                         and members)
Related Classes:
  Member, Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord, Date
Input:
  bookTitle : String - the title of the document to borrow. Must be
  non-null and non-empty.
  today     : Date   - the current borrowing date, used as the new
  record's borrowingDate. Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output:
  boolean - true when a BorrowRecord is successfully created and
  attached to this member; false otherwise (title missing, not a Book,
  already borrowed, or this member already holds three books).
Pre-condition:
  - bookTitle is non-null and non-empty.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
  - this.member is registered with a library whose documents are
    accessible.
Post-condition:
  - On success: this.borrowRecords size increases by exactly one and
    contains a new BorrowRecord whose borrowingDate is today and
    returnDue is exactly seven calendar days after today; returns true.
  - On failure: this.borrowRecords is unchanged; returns false.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary:
  Extends the due date of one of this member's borrowed books,
  identified by title, by delegating to the corresponding BorrowRecord.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty and that today
     is a valid Date.
  2. Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals
     bookTitle.
  3. If no such record exists, return the original due date of the
     record with that title? (per spec: when no record exists, return
     null or today's invariant; in this design we return null to signal
     "no record found").
     NOTE: Following the requirement literally, the operation acts on
     a member's own borrowed book; if there is no matching record, the
     due date is unaffected and we return null to indicate no change.
  4. Otherwise, invoke foundRecord.extendDueDate(today) and return the
     resulting Date (new due date when extended, or original due date
     when not eligible).
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read, iterated)
  - Calls BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Related Classes:
  Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document, Date
Input:
  bookTitle : String - the title of one of this member's borrowed
  books. Must be non-null and non-empty.
  today     : Date   - the current date used to evaluate eligibility.
  Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output:
  Date - the (possibly updated) due date returned by the matched
  BorrowRecord. If no matching record exists for this member, returns
  null. Returned in "yyyy-MM-dd" format when non-null.
Pre-condition:
  - bookTitle is non-null and non-empty.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - When a matching record exists and today >= borrowingDate AND
    today < returnDue, that record's returnDue is incremented by
    exactly seven days and that new value is returned.
  - When a matching record exists but the eligibility condition fails,
    the record's returnDue is unchanged and the original returnDue is
    returned.
  - When no matching record exists, no BorrowRecord is modified and
    null is returned.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary:
  Returns the list of titles of all books currently held by this member.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty List<String> result.
  2. For each BorrowRecord r in this.borrowRecords:
       a. If r.book is non-null, add r.book.title to result.
  3. Return result.
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read, iterated)
Related Classes:
  Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input:
  (none)
Output:
  List<String> - a list of book titles currently held by this member.
  The order follows the iteration order of this.borrowRecords. If this
  member has no active borrow records, returns an empty list.
Pre-condition:
  - this member is properly initialized with a (possibly empty)
    borrowRecords list.
Post-condition:
  - The state of this Member is not modified.
  - The returned list contains exactly the titles of books referenced
    by the current borrowRecords (skipping any null book references).

Member::returnBook
Summary:
  Allows this member to return a borrowed book identified by its title,
  removing the associated BorrowRecord on success.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty and that today
     is a valid Date.
  2. Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals
     bookTitle.
  3. If no such record exists, return false.
  4. If foundRecord.isOverdue(today) is true (i.e., today > returnDue),
     return false without modifying this.borrowRecords.
  5. Otherwise, remove foundRecord from this.borrowRecords.
  6. Return true.
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read and possibly modified)
  - Calls BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Related Classes:
  Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document, Date
Input:
  bookTitle : String - the title of the borrowed book to return. Must
  be non-null and non-empty.
  today     : Date   - the current date used to check whether the book
  is overdue. Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output:
  boolean - true when the matching BorrowRecord is successfully
  removed (member currently holds the title and it is not overdue);
  false otherwise (no matching record held by the member, or the book
  is overdue).
Pre-condition:
  - bookTitle is non-null and non-empty.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - On success: this.borrowRecords no longer contains the record
    associated with bookTitle; returns true.
  - On failure: this.borrowRecords is unchanged; returns false.

  1. Initialize an empty List<String> result.
  2. For each BorrowRecord r in this.borrowRecords:
       a. If r.book is non-null, add r.book.title to result.
  3. Return result.
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read, iterated)
Related Classes:
  Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input:
  (none)
Output:
  List<String> - a list of book titles currently held by this member.
  The order follows the iteration order of this.borrowRecords. If this
  member has no active borrow records, returns an empty list.
Pre-condition:
  - this member is properly initialized with a (possibly empty)
    borrowRecords list.
Post-condition:
  - The state of this Member is not modified.
  - The returned list contains exactly the titles of books referenced
    by the current borrowRecords (skipping any null book references).

Member::returnBook
Summary:
  Allows this member to return a borrowed book identified by its title,
  removing the associated BorrowRecord on success.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty and that today
     is a valid Date.
  2. Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals
     bookTitle.
  3. If no such record exists, return false.
  4. If foundRecord.isOverdue(today) is true (i.e., today > returnDue),
     return false without modifying this.borrowRecords.
  5. Otherwise, remove foundRecord from this.borrowRecords.
  6. Return true.
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read and possibly modified)
  - Calls BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Related Classes:
  Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document, Date
Input:
  bookTitle : String - the title of the borrowed book to return. Must
  be non-null and non-empty.
  today     : Date   - the current date used to check whether the book
  is overdue. Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output:
  boolean - true when the matching BorrowRecord is successfully
  removed (member currently holds the title and it is not overdue);
  false otherwise (no matching record held by the member, or the book
  is overdue).
Pre-condition:
  - bookTitle is non-null and non-empty.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - On success: this.borrowRecords no longer contains the record
    associated with bookTitle; returns true.
  - On failure: this.borrowRecords is unchanged; returns false.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml

    bookId : String
}

    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date

}

    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library

    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date

    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume

Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document

Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::addDocument
Summary:
  Adds a document to the library's document collection.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that the supplied doc is not null and that doc.title is
     not null or empty.
  2. Verify that no existing Document in this.documents has the same
     title (titles uniquely identify documents in the library).
  3. If both checks pass, append doc to this.documents.
  4. Otherwise, do nothing (the document is not added).
Related Features:
  - documents : List<Document>  (read and possibly appended)
Related Classes:
  Library, Document
Input:
  doc : Document - the document to register. Must be non-null with a
  non-null, non-empty title; must not duplicate an existing title in
  this.documents.
Output:
  void - no value is returned.
Pre-condition:
  - doc is not null.
  - doc.title is not null and not empty.
  - No existing entry in this.documents shares doc.title.
Post-condition:
  - If pre-conditions are satisfied, this.documents contains doc.
  - If any pre-condition fails, this.documents is unchanged.

Library::registerMember
Summary:
  Registers a new Member in the library given a first name and surname,
  enforcing uniqueness by full name and rejecting invalid input.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate firstName and surname: each must be non-null, non-empty,
     and not whitespace-only.
  2. Search this.members for any existing member whose
     (firstName + " " + surname) equals the candidate's full name.
  3. If validation passes and no duplicate full name exists:
       a. Create a new Member with the given firstName and surname,
          an empty borrowRecords list, and a back-reference to this
          Library.
       b. Append the new Member to this.members.
       c. Return true.
  4. Otherwise return false without modifying this.members.
Related Features:
  - members : List<Member>  (read and possibly appended)
Related Classes:
  Library, Member
Input:
  firstName : String - the member's first name. Must be non-null,
  non-empty, and not whitespace-only.
  surname   : String - the member's surname. Must be non-null,
  non-empty, and not whitespace-only.
Output:
  boolean - true if a new member is successfully registered; false
  otherwise (invalid input or duplicate full name).
Pre-condition:
  - firstName and surname are non-null, non-empty, non-whitespace.
  - No existing Member in this.members has exactly the same full name.
Post-condition:
  - On success: this.members gains a new Member with no borrowed
    books and a reference back to this Library; returns true.
  - On failure: this.members is unchanged; returns false.

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary:
  Determines whether the borrowed book associated with this record is
  overdue as of the supplied current date.
Algorithm:
  1. Receive the current date `today`.
  2. Return (today > returnDue).
Related Features:
  - returnDue : Date  (read)
Related Classes:
  BorrowRecord, Date
Input:
  today : Date - the current calendar date to compare against returnDue.
  Must be a valid date in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Output:
  boolean - true when today is strictly later than returnDue
  (i.e., the book is past its due date); false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
  - this.returnDue has been properly initialized.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - The state of this BorrowRecord is not modified.
  - The returned boolean correctly reflects whether today > returnDue.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary:
  Advances the return due date of this borrow record by exactly seven days
  when the extension request is valid, otherwise leaves it unchanged.
Algorithm:
  1. Receive the current date `today`.
  2. If today is on or after `borrowingDate` AND today is strictly before
     `returnDue`, then:
       a. Compute newDue = returnDue + 7 days (calendar days).
       b. Assign returnDue = newDue.
  3. Otherwise (today < borrowingDate, or today >= returnDue), keep
     returnDue unchanged.
  4. Return the (possibly updated) returnDue.
Related Features:
  - borrowingDate : Date  (read)
  - returnDue     : Date  (read and possibly written)
Related Classes:
  BorrowRecord, Date
Input:
  today : Date - the current calendar date used to evaluate extension
  eligibility. Must be a valid date in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Output:
  Date - the (possibly new) due date. When extended, it is exactly seven
  calendar days after the previous due date; otherwise it equals the
  original due date. Returned in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Pre-condition:
  - this.borrowingDate and this.returnDue have been properly initialized
    (returnDue equals borrowingDate + 7 days at creation time).
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - If the eligibility condition is satisfied, this.returnDue is
    incremented by exactly seven calendar days.
  - Otherwise this.returnDue is unchanged.
  - The returned Date equals the final value of this.returnDue.

You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary:
  Determines whether the borrowed book associated with this record is
  overdue as of the supplied current date.
Algorithm:
  1. Receive the current date `today`.
  2. Return (today > returnDue).
Related Features:
  - returnDue : Date  (read)
Related Classes:
  BorrowRecord, Date
Input:
  today : Date - the current calendar date to compare against returnDue.
  Must be a valid date in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Output:
  boolean - true when today is strictly later than returnDue
  (i.e., the book is past its due date); false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
  - this.returnDue has been properly initialized.
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - The state of this BorrowRecord is not modified.
  - The returned boolean correctly reflects whether today > returnDue.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary:
  Advances the return due date of this borrow record by exactly seven days
  when the extension request is valid, otherwise leaves it unchanged.
Algorithm:
  1. Receive the current date `today`.
  2. If today is on or after `borrowingDate` AND today is strictly before
     `returnDue`, then:
       a. Compute newDue = returnDue + 7 days (calendar days).
       b. Assign returnDue = newDue.
  3. Otherwise (today < borrowingDate, or today >= returnDue), keep
     returnDue unchanged.
  4. Return the (possibly updated) returnDue.
Related Features:
  - borrowingDate : Date  (read)
  - returnDue     : Date  (read and possibly written)
Related Classes:
  BorrowRecord, Date
Input:
  today : Date - the current calendar date used to evaluate extension
  eligibility. Must be a valid date in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Output:
  Date - the (possibly new) due date. When extended, it is exactly seven
  calendar days after the previous due date; otherwise it equals the
  original due date. Returned in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Pre-condition:
  - this.borrowingDate and this.returnDue have been properly initialized
    (returnDue equals borrowingDate + 7 days at creation time).
  - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Post-condition:
  - If the eligibility condition is satisfied, this.returnDue is
    incremented by exactly seven calendar days.
  - Otherwise this.returnDue is unchanged.
  - The returned Date equals the final value of this.returnDue.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Registers a new Member in the library given a first name and surname,
   *   enforcing uniqueness by full name and rejecting invalid input.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate firstName and surname: each must be non-null, non-empty,
   *      and not whitespace-only.
   *   2. Search this.members for any existing member whose
   *      (firstName + " " + surname) equals the candidate's full name.
   *   3. If validation passes and no duplicate full name exists:
   *        a. Create a new Member with the given firstName and surname,
   *           an empty borrowRecords list, and a back-reference to this
   *           Library.
   *        b. Append the new Member to this.members.
   *        c. Return true.
   *   4. Otherwise return false without modifying this.members.
   * Related Features:
   *   - members : List<Member>  (read and possibly appended)
   * Related Classes:
   *   Library, Member
   * Input:
   *   firstName : String - the member's first name. Must be non-null,
   *   non-empty, and not whitespace-only.
   *   surname   : String - the member's surname. Must be non-null,
   *   non-empty, and not whitespace-only.
   * Output:
   *   boolean - true if a new member is successfully registered; false
   *   otherwise (invalid input or duplicate full name).
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - firstName and surname are non-null, non-empty, non-whitespace.
   *   - No existing Member in this.members has exactly the same full name.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - On success: this.members gains a new Member with no borrowed
   *     books and a reference back to this Library; returns true.
   *   - On failure: this.members is unchanged; returns false.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean registerMember(final String firstName, final String surname)
  {
    if (firstName == null || firstName.trim().isEmpty() ||
        surname == null || surname.trim().isEmpty())
    {
      return false;
    }

    final String fullName = firstName + " " + surname;
    for (Member existing : getMembers())
    {
      final String existingFullName = existing.getFirstName() + " " + existing.getSurname();
      if (existingFullName.equals(fullName))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    final Member newMember = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createMember();
    newMember.setFirstName(firstName);
    newMember.setSurname(surname);
    newMember.setLibrary(this);
    getMembers().add(newMember);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Adds a document to the library's document collection.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate that the supplied doc is not null and that doc.title is
   *      not null or empty.
   *   2. Verify that no existing Document in this.documents has the same
   *      title (titles uniquely identify documents in the library).
   *   3. If both checks pass, append doc to this.documents.
   *   4. Otherwise, do nothing (the document is not added).
   * Related Features:
   *   - documents : List<Document>  (read and possibly appended)
   * Related Classes:
   *   Library, Document
   * Input:
   *   doc : Document - the document to register. Must be non-null with a
   *   non-null, non-empty title; must not duplicate an existing title in
   *   this.documents.
   * Output:
   *   void - no value is returned.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - doc is not null.
   *   - doc.title is not null and not empty.
   *   - No existing entry in this.documents shares doc.title.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - If pre-conditions are satisfied, this.documents contains doc.
   *   - If any pre-condition fails, this.documents is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void addDocument(final Document doc)
  {
    if (doc == null)
    {
      return;
    }

    final String title = doc.getTitle();
    if (title == null || title.isEmpty())
    {
      return;
    }

    for (Document existing : getDocuments())
    {
      if (title.equals(existing.getTitle()))
      {
        return;
      }
    }

    getDocuments().add(doc);
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   *      return false without modifying this.borrowRecords.
   *   5. Otherwise, remove foundRecord from this.borrowRecords.
   *   6. Return true.
   * Related Features:

   *   - Calls BorrowRecord::isOverdue
   * Related Classes:

   *   today     : Date   - the current date used to check whether the book
   *   is overdue. Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output:
   *   boolean - true when the matching BorrowRecord is successfully
   *   removed (member currently holds the title and it is not overdue);
   *   false otherwise (no matching record held by the member, or the book

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Extends the due date of one of this member's borrowed books,
   *   identified by title, by delegating to the corresponding BorrowRecord.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty and that today
   *      is a valid Date.
   *   2. Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals
   *      bookTitle.
   *   3. If no such record exists, return the original due date of the
   *      record with that title? (per spec: when no record exists, return
   *      null or today's invariant; in this design we return null to signal
   *      "no record found").
   *      NOTE: Following the requirement literally, the operation acts on
   *      a member's own borrowed book; if there is no matching record, the
   *      due date is unaffected and we return null to indicate no change.
   *   4. Otherwise, invoke foundRecord.extendDueDate(today) and return the
   *      resulting Date (new due date when extended, or original due date
   *      when not eligible).
   * Related Features:
   *   - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read, iterated)
   *   - Calls BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
   * Related Classes:
   *   Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document, Date
   * Input:
   *   bookTitle : String - the title of one of this member's borrowed
   *   books. Must be non-null and non-empty.
   *   today     : Date   - the current date used to evaluate eligibility.
   *   Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output:
   *   Date - the (possibly updated) due date returned by the matched
   *   BorrowRecord. If no matching record exists for this member, returns
   *   null. Returned in "yyyy-MM-dd" format when non-null.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - bookTitle is non-null and non-empty.
   *   - today is a valid Date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - When a matching record exists and today >= borrowingDate AND
   *     today < returnDue, that record's returnDue is incremented by
   *     exactly seven days and that new value is returned.
   *   - When a matching record exists but the eligibility condition fails,
   *     the record's returnDue is unchanged and the original returnDue is
   *     returned.
   *   - When no matching record exists, no BorrowRecord is modified and
   *     null is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated
   */

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * Summary:

   *   1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty, and that today

   *   boolean - true when the matching BorrowRecord is successfully

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * Summary:
   *   Allows this member to borrow a book identified by its title, creating
   *   a new BorrowRecord on success.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty, and that today
   *      is a valid Date.
   *   2. Look up the Document with the given bookTitle in
   *      this.library.documents. If not found, return false.
   *   3. If the located Document is not an instance of Book, return false.
   *   4. Check that no BorrowRecord currently exists in the library
   *      (i.e., across all Members' borrowRecords) referencing the same
   *      Book. If the book is already borrowed, return false.
   *   5. Check that this.borrowRecords.size() < 3. Otherwise return false.
   *   6. Create a new BorrowRecord with:
   *        borrowingDate = today,
   *        returnDue     = today + 7 days,
   *        book          = the located Book.
   *   7. Append the new BorrowRecord to this.borrowRecords.
   *   8. Return true.
   * Related Features:
   *   - borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>  (read and possibly appended)
   *   - library       : Library             (read, used to access documents
   *                                          and members)
   * Related Classes:
   *   Member, Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord, Date
   * Input:
   *   bookTitle : String - the title of the document to borrow. Must be
   *   non-null and non-empty.
   *   today     : Date   - the current borrowing date, used as the new
   *   record's borrowingDate. Must be a valid date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output:
   *   boolean - true when a BorrowRecord is successfully created and
   *   attached to this member; false otherwise (title missing, not a Book,
   *   already borrowed, or this member already holds three books).
   * Pre-condition:

   *     returnDue is exactly seven calendar days after today; returns true.
   *   - On failure: this.borrowRecords is unchanged; returns false.

  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    // Step 1: Validate inputs.
    if (bookTitle == null || bookTitle.isEmpty() || today == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    Library library = getLibrary();
    if (library == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: Look up the Document with the given bookTitle in this.library.documents.
    Document targetDoc = null;
    for (Document doc : library.getDocuments())
    {
      if (doc != null && bookTitle.equals(doc.getTitle()))
      {
        targetDoc = doc;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (targetDoc == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: If the located Document is not an instance of Book, return false.
    if (!(targetDoc instanceof Book))
    {
      return false;
    }
    Book book = (Book) targetDoc;

    // Step 4: Check that no BorrowRecord currently exists in the library referencing the same Book.
    for (Member m : library.getMembers())
    {
      for (BorrowRecord r : m.getBorrowRecords())
      {
        if (r.getBook() == book)
        {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    // Step 5: Check that this.borrowRecords.size() < 3.
    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 6: Create a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days, book = the located Book.
    BorrowRecord record = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    record.setBorrowingDate(today);

    Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal.setTime(today);
    cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, 7);
    Date returnDue = cal.getTime();
    record.setReturnDue(returnDue);
    record.setBook(book);

    // Step 7: Append the new BorrowRecord to this.borrowRecords.
    getBorrowRecords().add(record);

    // Step 8: Return true.
    return true;
  }

   * Summary:
   *   Allows this member to return a borrowed book identified by its title,
   *   removing the associated BorrowRecord on success.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Validate that bookTitle is non-null and non-empty and that today
   *      is a valid Date.
   *   2. Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals
   *      bookTitle.

   *   4. If foundRecord.isOverdue(today) is true (i.e., today > returnDue),
   *      return false without modifying this.borrowRecords.
   *   5. Otherwise, remove foundRecord from this.borrowRecords.
   *   6. Return true.
   * Related Features:

   *   - Calls BorrowRecord::isOverdue
   * Related Classes:

   * Output:
   *   boolean - true when the matching BorrowRecord is successfully
   *   removed (member currently holds the title and it is not overdue);
   *   false otherwise (no matching record held by the member, or the book

   *   - On failure: this.borrowRecords is unchanged; returns false.

  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    // Step 1: Validate inputs.
    if (bookTitle == null || bookTitle.isEmpty() || today == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: Search this.borrowRecords for the record whose book.title equals bookTitle.
    BorrowRecord foundRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord r : getBorrowRecords())
    {
      if (r.getBook() != null && bookTitle.equals(r.getBook().getTitle()))
      {
        foundRecord = r;
        break;
      }
    }

    // Step 3: If no such record exists, return false.
    if (foundRecord == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 4: If foundRecord.isOverdue(today) is true, return false without modifying this.borrowRecords.
    if (foundRecord.isOverdue(today))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 5: Otherwise, remove foundRecord from this.borrowRecords.
    getBorrowRecords().remove(foundRecord);

    // Step 6: Return true.
    return true;
  }

  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
